These models serve many purposes; this run probes exactly one narrow task: producing catalogue metadata for a single photograph from the assisted-lane prompt and whatever context it supplies — here, camera-recorded capture context plus draft descriptive hints previously produced by a more capable model. Results say nothing about a model's fitness for other uses.
